# Skills · I · part 18

913 entries, impeccable-ios to implement. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **impeccable-ios**: iOS and iPadOS platform conventions for native apps built with SwiftUI, UIKit, React Native, Expo, or Flutter. Use when building or reviewing iOS UI. Covers Human Interface Guidelines conformance as the governing rulebook, expressing brand through the layer the platform leaves open, and the iOS slop test for controls that read as ported from a website. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-ios) · ★4)
- **impeccable-ledger**: Runs chosen Impeccable commands (audit, critique, polish, distill, typeset, colorize, layout, bolder, quieter, animate, delight, overdrive, clarify, adapt, harden, onboard, optimize) over an existing UI in parallel, then produces an HTML decision ledger where every suggestion shows a coded before/after. The user approves, rejects, or requests changes per row and exports decisions.json. Feeding that back builds the approved rows (after a file backup), revises change requests, and re-reviews the whole UI in a new round. Use when the user invokes /impeccable-ledger, asks to "run impeccable on my… ([shubhdas0208/claude-skills](https://github.com/shubhdas0208/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/my-skills/impeccable-ledger))
